    position: fixed;
    top: 0;
    left: 0;
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);

    -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(15px);
    z-index: 99999;

</style><script>jQuery(function ($) {
    if (!$('body').hasClass('single-product')) return;
    const $form = $('form.cart');
    if (!$form.length) return;
    
    // Ensure the cursor always stays as a pointer, never the red "no" sign
    $('.single_add_to_cart_button').css('cursor', 'pointer');
    
    $form.on('submit', function (e) {
        /**
         * 1. CONFLICT CHECK:
         * Skip this AJAX script if a "Buy Now" button was clicked.
         */
        const $activeElement = $(document.activeElement);
        if ($activeElement.hasClass('sbw-skip-ajax') || $activeElement.attr('name') === 'sbw-buy-now-action') {
            return; 
        }
        
        const $btn = $form.find('.single_add_to_cart_button');
        const $varInput = $form.find('input[name="variation_id"]');
        
        /**
         * 2. CLICKABLE VALIDATION:
         * If no option is selected, show an alert. 
         * The button remains clickable and the cursor remains a standard pointer.
         */
        if ($form.hasClass('variations_form')) {
            const varId = $varInput.val();
            if (!varId || varId == "0" || varId === "") {
                e.preventDefault(); 
                alert("Please select an option before adding to cart!");
                return false;
            }
        }
        
        e.preventDefault();
        
        // NEW: Prepare data properly to avoid duplication
        const product_id = $form.find('input[name="product_id"], button[name="add-to-cart"]').val();
        const variation_id = $varInput.val() || 0;
        const quantity = $form.find('input[name="quantity"]').val() || 1;
        
        // Collect variation attributes if it's a variable product
        const variation = {};
        if ($form.hasClass('variations_form')) {
            $form.find('select[name^="attribute_"], input[name^="attribute_"]:checked, input[name^="attribute_"][type="hidden"]').each(function() {
                const name = $(this).attr('name');
                const value = $(this).val();
                if (name && value) {
                    variation[name] = value;
                }
            });
        }
        
        $btn.addClass('loading');
        
        // NEW: Use proper data structure for the AJAX call
        $.ajax({
            url: SBQA.ajax,
            type: 'POST',
            data: {
                action: 'sbqa_add_to_cart',
                nonce: SBQA.nonce,
                product_id: product_id,
                variation_id: variation_id,
                quantity: quantity,
                variation: variation
            },
            success: function (resp) {
                if (resp && resp.success) {
                    $('.woocommerce-error, .woocommerce-message, .woocommerce-info').remove();
                    
                    if (resp.data && resp.data.html) {
                        $('#sbqa-cart-body').html(resp.data.html);
                    }
                    
                    $(document.body).trigger('wc_fragment_refresh');
                    
                    // Open the drawer
                    if (typeof openDrawer === 'function') {
                        openDrawer();
                    } else {
                        $('#sbqa-overlay').addClass('is-open');
                        $('#sbqa-drawer').addClass('is-open').attr('aria-hidden', 'false');
                        $('body').addClass('sbqa-lock');
                    }
                } else {
                    alert(resp.data && resp.data.message ? resp.data.message : 'Unable to add to cart.');
                }
            },
            error: function() {
                alert('An error occurred. Please try again.');
            },
            complete: function () {
                $btn.removeClass('loading');
            }
        });
    });
});</script><style class="wpcode-css-snippet">/* ===== SBQA Single Product Variation Buttons ===== */
.sbqa-single-variations .variations select {
  display: none !important;
}
.sbqa-single-variations .sbqa-variation-group {
  margin: 14px 0;
}
.sbqa-single-variations .sbqa-variation-label {
  font-weight: 800;
  margin-bottom: 10px;
}
.sbqa-single-variations .sbqa-variation-options {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 10px;
}
/* BUTTON BASE */
.sbqa-single-variations .sbqa-var-btn {
  padding: 10px 14px;
  border-radius: 12px;
  border: 1px solid rgba(0,0,0,.2);
  background: #fff;
  font-weight: 800;
  cursor: pointer;
  color: #0f172a !important;
  text-transform: none !important;
  transition: background .15s ease, color .15s ease, border-color .15s ease;
}
/* HOVER FIX */
.sbqa-single-variations .sbqa-var-btn:hover {
  background: #f1f5f9;
  color: #0f172a !important;
  border-color: #0f172a;
}
/* ACTIVE */
.sbqa-single-variations .sbqa-var-btn.active {
  background: #0f172a;
  color: #ffffff !important;
  border-color: #0f172a;
}
/* OUT OF STOCK */
.sbqa-single-variations .sbqa-var-btn.oos {
  opacity: .45;
  text-decoration: line-through;
}
/* OOS HOVER (still readable) */
.sbqa-single-variations .sbqa-var-btn.oos:hover {
  background: #ffffff;
  color: #0f172a !important;
}
/* REMOVE STOCK MESSAGE COMPLETELY */
.sbqa-single-variations .sbqa-stock-msg {
  display: none !important;
}
/* Hide default Woo variation labels (prevents duplicate "Size") */
.sbqa-single-variations .variations td.label {
  display: none !important;
}
/* Hide default Woo variation labels (Astra/Woo can use th.label OR td.label) */
.sbqa-single-variations table.variations th.label,
.sbqa-single-variations table.variations td.label{
  display:none !important;
}
/* Optional: remove left spacing after hiding label column */
.sbqa-single-variations table.variations td.value,
.sbqa-single-variations table.variations th.value{
  padding-left:0 !important;
}</style><script>jQuery(function ($) {
  $('.variations_form').each(function () {
    const $form = $(this);
    const variations = $form.data('product_variations') || [];

    /* 1. Build STOCK MAP */
    const stockMap = {};
    variations.forEach(v => {
      if (!v.is_in_stock) return;
      Object.entries(v.attributes).forEach(([k, val]) => {
        if (!stockMap[k]) stockMap[k] = {};
        stockMap[k][val] = true;
      });
    });

    /* 2. Replace SELECTS with BUTTONS */
    $form.find('select').each(function () {
      const $select = $(this);
      const name = $select.attr('name');
      const defaultValue = $select.val(); // Get WooCommerce default selection

      const $wrap = $('<div class="sbqa-variation-group"></div>');
      const label = $select.closest('tr').find('label').text();
      $wrap.append('<div class="sbqa-variation-label">' + label + '</div>');

      const $opts = $('<div class="sbqa-variation-options"></div>');

      $select.find('option').each(function () {
        const val = this.value;
        if (!val) return;

        const inStock = stockMap[name] && stockMap[name][val];
        const $btn = $('<button type="button" class="sbqa-var-btn">' + this.text + '</button>');

        if (!inStock) $btn.addClass('oos');

        // ✅ AUTO-ACTIVE: Check if this option is the default selection
        if (val === defaultValue) {
          $btn.addClass('active');
        }

        $btn.on('click', function () {
          $opts.find('.sbqa-var-btn').removeClass('active');
          $btn.addClass('active');
          $select.val(val).trigger('change');
          updateButtonState();
        });

        $opts.append($btn);
      });

      $wrap.append($opts);
      $select.after($wrap);
    });

    /* 3. Button State & Click Alert Logic */
    function updateButtonState() {
      const selected = {};
      $form.find('select').each(function () {
        selected[this.name] = this.value;
      });

      const complete = Object.values(selected).every(v => v);
      const match = variations.find(v =>
        Object.entries(selected).every(([k, val]) => v.attributes[k] === val)
      );

      const $addBtn = $form.find('.single_add_to_cart_button:not(.sbw-skip-ajax)');
      const $buyNowBtn = $form.find('.sbw-skip-ajax');

      // ✅ FORCE CLICKABLE: Remove disabled and red cursor
      $addBtn.prop('disabled', false).css('cursor', 'pointer');
      if ($buyNowBtn.length) $buyNowBtn.prop('disabled', false).css('cursor', 'pointer');

      // Update text logic
      if (!complete) {
        $addBtn.text('Add to cart');
      } else if (!match || !match.is_in_stock) {
        $addBtn.text('Sold Out');
      } else {
        $addBtn.text('Add to cart');
      }
    }

    // ✅ ALERT INTERCEPTION: Trigger alert if not selected
    $form.on('click', '.single_add_to_cart_button, .sbw-skip-ajax', function (e) {
      const selected = {};
      $form.find('select').each(function () {
        selected[this.name] = this.value;
      });

      const complete = Object.values(selected).every(v => v);

      if (!complete) {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopImmediatePropagation();
        alert("Please select an option first!");
        return false;
      }
    });

    $form.on('woocommerce_variation_has_changed', updateButtonState);
    updateButtonState();
  });
});</script></head>

					const lazyloadBackgrounds = document.querySelectorAll( `.e-con.e-parent:not(.e-lazyloaded)` );
					const lazyloadBackgroundObserver = new IntersectionObserver( ( entries ) => {
						entries.forEach( ( entry ) => {
							if ( entry.isIntersecting ) {
								let lazyloadBackground = entry.target;
								if( lazyloadBackground ) {
									lazyloadBackground.classList.add( 'e-lazyloaded' );
								}
								lazyloadBackgroundObserver.unobserve( entry.target );
							}
						});
					}, { rootMargin: '200px 0px 200px 0px' } );
					lazyloadBackgrounds.forEach( ( lazyloadBackground ) => {
						lazyloadBackgroundObserver.observe( lazyloadBackground );
					} );
				};
				const events = [
					'DOMContentLoaded',
					'elementor/lazyload/observe',
				];
				events.forEach( ( event ) => {
					document.addEventListener( event, lazyloadRunObserver );
				} );

<script id="woo-search-script-js-after">
jQuery(document).ready(function($) {
    let searchTimeout;
    
    // Open search overlay
    $(document).on('click', '.woo-search-trigger', function(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        $('.woo-search-overlay').addClass('active');
        $('body').css('overflow', 'hidden');
        setTimeout(function() {
            $('.woo-search-input').focus();
        }, 300);
    });
    
    // Close search overlay
    $(document).on('click', '.woo-search-close, .woo-search-overlay', function(e) {
        if (e.target === this) {
            $('.woo-search-overlay').removeClass('active');
            $('body').css('overflow', '');
            $('.woo-search-input').val('');
            $('.woo-search-results').html('');
        }
    });
    
    // Close on ESC key
    $(document).on('keydown', function(e) {
        if (e.key === 'Escape' && $('.woo-search-overlay').hasClass('active')) {
            $('.woo-search-overlay').removeClass('active');
            $('body').css('overflow', '');
            $('.woo-search-input').val('');
            $('.woo-search-results').html('');
        }
    });
    
    // AJAX Search
    $(document).on('input', '.woo-search-input', function() {
        const query = $(this).val().trim();
        
        clearTimeout(searchTimeout);
        
        if (query.length < 2) {
            $('.woo-search-results').html('');
            $('.woo-search-loading').hide();
            return;
        }
        
        $('.woo-search-loading').show();
        
        searchTimeout = setTimeout(function() {
            $.ajax({
                url: WooSearch.ajax_url,
                type: 'POST',
                data: {
                    action: 'woo_ajax_search',
                    nonce: WooSearch.nonce,
                    query: query
                },
                success: function(response) {
                    $('.woo-search-loading').hide();
                    if (response.success) {
                        $('.woo-search-results').html(response.data.html);
                    } else {
                        $('.woo-search-results').html('<div class="woo-search-no-results"><p>No products found</p></div>');
                    }
                },
                error: function() {
                    $('.woo-search-loading').hide();
                    $('.woo-search-results').html('<div class="woo-search-no-results"><p>Search error. Please try again.</p></div>');
                }
            });
        }, 300);
    });
    
    // Prevent overlay close when clicking inside container
    $(document).on('click', '.woo-search-container', function(e) {
        e.stopPropagation();
    });
});

//# sourceURL=woo-search-script-js-after
</script>

<script id="sbqa-js-extra">
var SBQA = {"ajax":"https://anchorpeptide.com/wp-admin/admin-ajax.php","nonce":"58d53f8b1a","checkoutUrl":"https://anchorpeptide.com/checkout/","freeShippingThreshold":"250","shippingCost":"0"};
//# sourceURL=sbqa-js-extra

<script id="sbqa-js-after">
(function($){
  var $body = $('body');
  var FREE_SHIPPING_THRESHOLD = parseFloat(SBQA.freeShippingThreshold) || 250;
  var SHIPPING_COST = parseFloat(SBQA.shippingCost) || 0;

  function lock(){ $body.addClass('sbqa-lock'); }
  function unlock(){ $body.removeClass('sbqa-lock'); }

  function openDrawer(){
    $('#sbqa-overlay').addClass('is-open');
    $('#sbqa-drawer').addClass('is-open').attr('aria-hidden','false');
    lock();
  }
  function closeDrawer(){
    $('#sbqa-overlay').removeClass('is-open');
    $('#sbqa-drawer').removeClass('is-open').attr('aria-hidden','true');
    unlock();
  }

  function openModal(){
    $('#sbqa-var-overlay').addClass('is-open');
    $('#sbqa-var-modal').addClass('is-open').attr('aria-hidden','false');
    lock();
  }
  function closeModal(){
    $('#sbqa-var-overlay').removeClass('is-open');
    $('#sbqa-var-modal').removeClass('is-open').attr('aria-hidden','true');
    $('#sbqa-var-body').html('');
    unlock();
  }

  $(document).on('keydown', function(e){
    if(e.key === 'Escape'){
      closeModal();
      closeDrawer();
    }
  });

  $(document).on('click', '#sbqa-overlay', closeDrawer);
  $(document).on('click', '#sbqa-var-overlay', closeModal);

  $(document).on('click', '.sbqa-close', function(){
    closeModal();
    closeDrawer();
  });

  function updateShippingBar(cartTotal){
    var $msg      = $('#sbqa-shipping-msg');
    var $progress = $('#sbqa-shipping-progress');

    if(!$msg.length || !$progress.length) return;

    var total      = parseFloat(cartTotal) || 0;
    var remaining  = FREE_SHIPPING_THRESHOLD - total;
    var percentage = Math.min((total / FREE_SHIPPING_THRESHOLD) * 100, 100);

    $progress.css('width', percentage + '%');

    if(remaining <= 0){
      $msg.html('You qualify for <strong>FREE SHIPPING!</strong>');
      $msg.removeClass('sbqa-ship-pending').addClass('sbqa-ship-done');
    } else {
      $msg.html('Add <strong>$' + remaining.toFixed(2) + '</strong> more to get <strong>FREE SHIPPING</strong>');
      $msg.removeClass('sbqa-ship-done').addClass('sbqa-ship-pending');
    }

    var $shipRow = $('#sbqa-shipping-row');
    var $shipAmt = $('#sbqa-shipping-amount');

    if(total <= 0){
      $shipRow.hide();
    } else if(remaining <= 0){
      $shipRow.show();
      $shipAmt.html('<span class="sbqa-free-badge">FREE</span>');
    } else {
      $shipRow.show();
      if(SHIPPING_COST > 0){
        $shipAmt.text('$' + SHIPPING_COST.toFixed(2));
      } else {
        $shipAmt.text('Calculated at checkout');
      }
    }
  }

  function setCartHtml(html){
    $('#sbqa-cart-body').html(html || '');
  }

  function refreshDrawer(openAfter){
    $.post(SBQA.ajax, { action:'sbqa_get_cart', nonce:SBQA.nonce }, function(resp){
      if(resp && resp.success){
        setCartHtml(resp.data.html);
        if(resp.data.cart_total !== undefined){
          updateShippingBar(resp.data.cart_total);
        }
        if(openAfter) openDrawer();
      }
    });
  }

  function ajaxAddToCart(payload, openAfter){
    payload = payload || {};
    payload.action = 'sbqa_add_to_cart';
    payload.nonce  = SBQA.nonce;

    return $.post(SBQA.ajax, payload, function(resp){
      if(resp && resp.success){
        setCartHtml(resp.data.html);
        if(resp.data.cart_total !== undefined){
          updateShippingBar(resp.data.cart_total);
        }
        if(openAfter) openDrawer();
        $(document.body).trigger('wc_fragment_refresh');
      } else {
        alert((resp && resp.data && resp.data.message) ? resp.data.message : 'Unable to add to cart.');
      }
    });
  }

  function ajaxAddSimple(product_id, qty){
    qty = qty || 1;
    return ajaxAddToCart({
      product_id: product_id,
      quantity: qty
    }, true);
  }

  function escapeHtml(str){
    return String(str == null ? '' : str).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function(m){
      return ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#039;'}[m]);
    });
  }

  function escapeTextarea(str){
    return String(str == null ? '' : str).replace(/<\/textarea/gi, '<\\/textarea');
  }

  function getSelectedAttributes(){
    var selected = {};
    $('#sbqa-var-modal .sbqa-attr').each(function(){
      var attrKey = $(this).data('attr');
      var $active = $(this).find('.sbqa-pill.is-active');
      selected[attrKey] = $active.length ? String($active.data('value')) : '';
    });
    return selected;
  }

  function isCompleteSelection(){
    var complete = true;
    $('#sbqa-var-modal .sbqa-attr').each(function(){
      if($(this).find('.sbqa-pill.is-active').length === 0) complete = false;
    });
    return complete;
  }

  function findMatchedInStockVariation(){
    var json = ($('#sbqa-var-json').val() || '').trim();
    var vars = [];
    try { vars = JSON.parse(json || '[]'); } catch(e){ vars = []; }

    var selected = getSelectedAttributes();

    for(var i=0;i<vars.length;i++){
      var v = vars[i];
      if(!v || !v.attributes) continue;
      if(!v.is_in_stock) continue;

      var ok = true;
      for(var k in selected){
        var need = selected[k];
        var have = v.attributes[k] || '';

        if(!need) { ok = false; break; }
        if(String(have) !== String(need)) { ok = false; break; }
      }
      if(ok) return v;
    }
    return null;
  }

  function findMatchedAnyVariation(){
    var json = ($('#sbqa-var-json').val() || '').trim();
    var vars = [];
    try { vars = JSON.parse(json || '[]'); } catch(e){ vars = []; }

    var selected = getSelectedAttributes();

    for(var i=0;i<vars.length;i++){
      var v = vars[i];
      if(!v || !v.attributes) continue;

      var ok = true;
      for(var k in selected){
        var need = selected[k];
        var have = v.attributes[k] || '';

        if(!need) { ok = false; break; }
        if(String(have) !== String(need)) { ok = false; break; }
      }
      if(ok) return v;
    }
    return null;
  }

  function updateAddToCartButtonPrice(){
    var $btn = $('#sbqa-var-modal .sbqa-var-add');
    if(!$btn.length) return;

    var btnStyle = 'display:block!important;width:100%!important;padding:14px 18px!important;font-size:15px!important;font-weight:800!important;border-radius:12px!important;background:#000!important;color:#fff!important;border:none!important;cursor:pointer!important;text-align:center!important;box-sizing:border-box!important;';

    var complete = isCompleteSelection();
    if(!complete){
      $btn.attr('style', btnStyle).text('Add to cart');
      return;
    }

    var match = findMatchedInStockVariation();
    if(match && match.variation_id && match.price_num){
      var formatted = '$' + parseFloat(match.price_num).toFixed(2);
      $btn.attr('style', btnStyle).text('Add to cart \u2014 ' + formatted);
      return;
    }

    $btn.attr('style', btnStyle).text('Add to cart');
  }

  function setStockUI(){
    var complete = isCompleteSelection();
    var $msg = $('#sbqa-var-modal .sbqa-var-stock-msg');

    if(!complete){
      $msg.hide().text('');
      updateAddToCartButtonPrice();
      return;
    }

    var inStockMatch = findMatchedInStockVariation();
    if(inStockMatch && inStockMatch.variation_id){
      $msg.hide().text('');

      if(inStockMatch.price_html){
        $('#sbqa-var-modal .sbqa-var-price').html(inStockMatch.price_html);
      }
      updateAddToCartButtonPrice();
      return;
    }

    var anyMatch = findMatchedAnyVariation();

    if(anyMatch && anyMatch.variation_id && anyMatch.is_in_stock === false){
      $msg.show().text('Out of stock');
      if(anyMatch.price_html){
        $('#sbqa-var-modal .sbqa-var-price').html(anyMatch.price_html);
      }
    } else {
      $msg.show().text('Out of stock');
    }
    updateAddToCartButtonPrice();
  }

  function renderVariableModal(d){
    var attrs        = d.attributes || {};
    var keys         = Object.keys(attrs);
    var optionStock  = d.option_stock || {};
    var defaultAttrs = d.default_attributes || {};

    var html = '';

    html += '<div class="sbqa-var-head sbqa-var-head-grid">';
    html += '  <div class="sbqa-var-img">';
    if(d.image){
      html += '    <img src="'+escapeHtml(d.image)+'" alt="'+escapeHtml(d.name)+'" />';
    }
    html += '  </div>';
    html += '  <div class="sbqa-var-head-meta">';
    html += '    <div class="sbqa-var-title">'+escapeHtml(d.name)+'</div>';
    if(d.price_html) html += '    <div class="sbqa-var-price">'+d.price_html+'</div>';
    html += '  </div>';
    html += '</div>';

    if(keys.length === 0){
      html += '<div class="sbqa-error">No selectable options found.</div>';
      $('#sbqa-var-body').html(html);
      return;
    }

    html += '<div class="sbqa-var-attrs">';

    keys.forEach(function(attrKey){
      var group        = attrs[attrKey];
      var label        = group.label || attrKey;
      var options      = group.options || [];
      var defaultValue = defaultAttrs[attrKey] || '';

      html += '<div class="sbqa-attr" data-attr="'+escapeHtml(attrKey)+'">';
      html += '  <div class="sbqa-attr-label">'+escapeHtml(label)+'</div>';
      html += '  <div class="sbqa-pill-wrap">';

      options.forEach(function(optObj){
        var val         = String(optObj.value == null ? '' : optObj.value);
        var lab         = String(optObj.label == null ? optObj.value : optObj.label);
        var inStock     = !!(optionStock[attrKey] && optionStock[attrKey][val]);
        var isDefault   = (defaultValue && String(defaultValue).toLowerCase() === val.toLowerCase());
        var activeClass = isDefault ? ' is-active' : '';
        var cls         = inStock
          ? ('sbqa-pill' + activeClass)
          : ('sbqa-pill sbqa-pill-oos' + activeClass);

        html += '<button type="button" class="'+cls+'" data-value="'+escapeHtml(val)+'">'+escapeHtml(lab)+'</button>';
      });

      html += '  </div>';
      html += '</div>';
    });

    html += '</div>';

    html += '<div class="sbqa-var-stock-msg" style="display:none" aria-live="polite"></div>';
    html += '<div class="sbqa-var-error-msg" style="display:none;color:#b91c1c;font-weight:800;font-size:13px;margin-top:10px;" aria-live="polite">Please select an option first</div>';

    html += '<div class="sbqa-var-actions">';
    html += '  <button type="button" class="sbqa-var-add" style="display:block!important;width:100%!important;padding:14px 18px!important;font-size:15px!important;font-weight:800!important;border-radius:12px!important;background:#000!important;color:#fff!important;border:none!important;cursor:pointer!important;text-align:center!important;box-sizing:border-box!important;">Add to cart</button>';
    html += '</div>';

    html += '<textarea id="sbqa-var-json" style="display:none !important;">'+escapeTextarea(JSON.stringify(d.variations))+'</textarea>';

    $('#sbqa-var-body').html(html);

    setTimeout(function(){
      setStockUI();
    }, 0);
  }

  function loadVariableModal(product_id){
    $('#sbqa-var-body').html('<div class="sbqa-loading">Loading options...</div>');
    $('#sbqa-var-modal').attr('data-product_id', product_id);
    openModal();

    $.post(SBQA.ajax, { action:'sbqa_get_variable_data', nonce:SBQA.nonce, product_id:product_id }, function(resp){
      if(!resp || !resp.success){
        $('#sbqa-var-body').html('<div class="sbqa-error">Unable to load options.</div>');
        return;
      }
      renderVariableModal(resp.data);
    });
  }

  $(document).on('click', '#sbqa-var-modal .sbqa-pill', function(){
    var $pill  = $(this);
    var $group = $pill.closest('.sbqa-attr');

    if($pill.hasClass('is-active')){
      $pill.removeClass('is-active');
    } else {
      $group.find('.sbqa-pill').removeClass('is-active');
      $pill.addClass('is-active');
    }

    $('#sbqa-var-modal .sbqa-var-error-msg').hide();

    setStockUI();
  });

  $(document).on('click', '#sbqa-var-modal .sbqa-var-add', function(){
    var pid = parseInt($('#sbqa-var-modal').attr('data-product_id'), 10);
    if(!pid) return;

    var complete = isCompleteSelection();
    if(!complete){
      $('#sbqa-var-modal .sbqa-var-error-msg').show();
      return;
    }

    var match = findMatchedInStockVariation();
    if(!match || !match.variation_id){
      return;
    }

    var selected = getSelectedAttributes();

    ajaxAddToCart({
      product_id:   pid,
      quantity:     1,
      variation_id: match.variation_id,
      variation:    selected
    }, true);

    closeModal();
  });

  $(document).on('click', '.sbqa-quick-add', function(e){
    e.preventDefault();
    var pid  = parseInt($(this).data('product_id'), 10);
    var type = String($(this).data('product_type') || '');

    if(!pid) return;

    if(type === 'variable'){
      $.post(SBQA.ajax, { action:'sbqa_get_variable_data', nonce:SBQA.nonce, product_id:pid }, function(resp){
        if(!resp || !resp.success){
          loadVariableModal(pid);
          return;
        }

        var d    = resp.data || {};
        var vars = Array.isArray(d.variations) ? d.variations : [];

        if(vars.length === 1 && vars[0] && vars[0].variation_id){
          var v = vars[0];

          if(v.is_in_stock){
            var variationPayload = v.attributes || {};
            ajaxAddToCart({
              product_id:   pid,
              quantity:     1,
              variation_id: v.variation_id,
              variation:    variationPayload
            }, true);
            return;
          }

          $('#sbqa-var-body').html('<div class="sbqa-loading">Loading options...</div>');
          $('#sbqa-var-modal').attr('data-product_id', pid);
          openModal();
          renderVariableModal(d);
          setTimeout(setStockUI, 0);
          return;
        }

        $('#sbqa-var-body').html('<div class="sbqa-loading">Loading options...</div>');
        $('#sbqa-var-modal').attr('data-product_id', pid);
        openModal();
        renderVariableModal(d);
        setTimeout(setStockUI, 0);
      });
    } else {
      ajaxAddSimple(pid, 1);
    }
  });

  $(document).on('click', '.sbqa-qty-btn', function(){
    var key    = $(this).data('key');
    var delta  = parseInt($(this).data('delta'), 10);
    var $row   = $(this).closest('.sbqa-item');
    var $qtyVal = $row.find('.sbqa-qty-val');
    var qty    = parseInt($qtyVal.text(), 10) || 1;

    qty = qty + delta;
    if(qty < 0) qty = 0;

    if(qty === 0){
      $row.fadeOut(200, function(){ $(this).remove(); });
    } else {
      $qtyVal.text(qty);
    }

    $.post(SBQA.ajax, { action:'sbqa_update_qty', nonce:SBQA.nonce, cart_item_key:key, qty:qty }, function(resp){
      if(resp && resp.success){
        setCartHtml(resp.data.html);
        if(resp.data.cart_total !== undefined){
          updateShippingBar(resp.data.cart_total);
        }
        $(document.body).trigger('wc_fragment_refresh');
      }
    });
  });

  $(document).on('click', '.sbqa-remove-btn', function(){
    var key  = $(this).data('key');
    var $row = $(this).closest('.sbqa-item');

    $row.fadeOut(200, function(){ $(this).remove(); });

    $.post(SBQA.ajax, { action:'sbqa_remove_item', nonce:SBQA.nonce, cart_item_key:key }, function(resp){
      if(resp && resp.success){
        setCartHtml(resp.data.html);
        if(resp.data.cart_total !== undefined){
          updateShippingBar(resp.data.cart_total);
        }
        $(document.body).trigger('wc_fragment_refresh');
      }
    });
  });

  /* NEW: Coupon toggle */
  $(document).on('click', '#sbqa-coupon-toggle', function(){
    var $panel = $('#sbqa-coupon-panel');
    var $arrow = $(this).find('.sbqa-coupon-arrow');
    if($panel.is(':visible')){
      $panel.slideUp(180);
      $arrow.css('transform','rotate(0deg)');
    } else {
      $panel.slideDown(180);
      $arrow.css('transform','rotate(180deg)');
      setTimeout(function(){ $('#sbqa-coupon-input').focus(); }, 200);
    }
  });

  /* NEW: Coupon apply */
  $(document).on('click', '#sbqa-coupon-apply', function(){
    var code = $('#sbqa-coupon-input').val().trim();
    if(!code) return;
    var $btn = $(this);
    var $msg = $('#sbqa-coupon-msg');
    $btn.prop('disabled', true).text('Applying...');
    $msg.hide().text('').removeClass('sbqa-coupon-ok sbqa-coupon-err');
    $.post(SBQA.ajax, { action:'sbqa_apply_coupon', nonce:SBQA.nonce, coupon_code:code }, function(resp){
      $btn.prop('disabled', false).text('Apply');
      if(resp && resp.success){
        $('#sbqa-coupon-input').val('');
        $msg.addClass('sbqa-coupon-ok').text(resp.data.message || 'Coupon applied!').show();
        setCartHtml(resp.data.html);
        if(resp.data.cart_total !== undefined) updateShippingBar(resp.data.cart_total);
        $(document.body).trigger('wc_fragment_refresh');
      } else {
        var err = (resp && resp.data && resp.data.message) ? resp.data.message : 'Invalid coupon code.';
        $msg.addClass('sbqa-coupon-err').text(err).show();
      }
    }).fail(function(){
      $btn.prop('disabled', false).text('Apply');
      $msg.addClass('sbqa-coupon-err').text('Something went wrong. Please try again.').show();
    });
  });

  $(document).on('keydown', '#sbqa-coupon-input', function(e){
    if(e.key === 'Enter'){ e.preventDefault(); $('#sbqa-coupon-apply').trigger('click'); }
  });

  /* NEW: Coupon remove tag */
  $(document).on('click', '.sbqa-coupon-remove', function(){
    var code = $(this).data('coupon');
    if(!code) return;
    $.post(SBQA.ajax, { action:'sbqa_remove_coupon', nonce:SBQA.nonce, coupon_code:code }, function(resp){
      if(resp && resp.success){
        setCartHtml(resp.data.html);
        if(resp.data.cart_total !== undefined) updateShippingBar(resp.data.cart_total);
        $(document.body).trigger('wc_fragment_refresh');
      }
    });
  });

  $(function(){
    refreshDrawer(false);
  });

})(jQuery);
(function($){
  function openDrawer(){
    $('#sbqa-overlay').addClass('is-open');
    $('#sbqa-drawer').addClass('is-open').attr('aria-hidden','false');
    $('body').addClass('sbqa-lock');
  }

  function setBadge(count){
    count = parseInt(count, 10) || 0;
    $('.sbqa-cart-badge').each(function(){
      const $b = $(this);
      if(count > 0){
        $b.text(count).attr('data-count', count).show();
      } else {
        $b.text('0').attr('data-count', 0).hide();
      }
    });
  }

  // Click icon -> open sidebar cart immediately, refresh content in background
  $(document).on('click', '.sbqa-cart-icon-btn', function(e){
    e.preventDefault();
    // Open drawer right away — no waiting
    openDrawer();
    // Then silently refresh cart content in the background
    if(window.SBQA && SBQA.ajax && SBQA.nonce){
      $.post(SBQA.ajax, { action:'sbqa_get_cart', nonce:SBQA.nonce }, function(resp){
        if(resp && resp.success && resp.data && typeof resp.data.html !== 'undefined'){
          $('#sbqa-cart-body').html(resp.data.html || '');
        }
      });
    }
  });

  // Update badge when Woo fragments refresh
  $(document.body).on('wc_fragments_refreshed wc_fragment_refresh added_to_cart', function(){
    let count = 0;
    const $mini = $('.cart-contents .count, .ast-icon-shopping-cart + .count, .header-cart-count, .cart-count');
    if($mini.length){
      const t = ($mini.first().text() || '').replace(/[^\d]/g,'');
      if(t) count = parseInt(t,10) || 0;
    }
    if(!count){
      $('#sbqa-cart-body .sbqa-qty-val').each(function(){
        count += parseInt($(this).text(), 10) || 0;
      });
    }
    setBadge(count);
  });

  $(function(){
    const first = $('.sbqa-cart-badge').first();
    if(first.length){
      setBadge(first.data('count'));
    }
  });

})(jQuery);
